# Agents · S · part 37

1,057 entries, source-searcher to spec-analyst. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **source-searcher**: Searches one work source for the second brain and returns sourced findings. Use for parallel searches across Outlook, SharePoint, OneDrive, Teams, Slack, Google Drive, and local files. ([ArcInfo-Consulting/second-brain](https://github.com/ArcInfo-Consulting/second-brain/blob/HEAD/plugins/second-brain/agents/source-searcher.md))
- **source-searcher**: Take a search plan from topic-scoper and execute it across all enabled sources. Return a clean, deduplicated, ranked list of candidates ready for the `paper-analyzer` agent. The source-searcher is the bridge between planning and analysis — it does not judge paper quality deeply, but it does screen and rank. ([ryypow/claude-kit](https://github.com/ryypow/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/deep-research/agents/source-searcher.md))
- **source-semantics-advisor**: Advises on source-specific runtime semantics — cursor strategies, threads / parent-child message handling, volume caps, onboarding-mode initial scope, and the `_sources.json` lookup-before-write protocol. Engage when designing the cursor shape for a new source, debugging duplicate entities, or deciding whether the source needs a tracked-parent registry. ([AgntUX/AUX-plugins](https://github.com/AgntUX/AUX-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/agntux-build/agents/source-semantics-advisor.md))
- **source-set-guide** ([habibbrox2/DoraiAi](https://github.com/habibbrox2/DoraiAi/blob/HEAD/agents/source-set-guide.md))
- **source-setup-copilot**: You help operators set up a source safely for preview acquisition in `platform-control`. ([philipplukas/evidara](https://github.com/philipplukas/evidara/blob/HEAD/platform-control/retool/agents/source-setup-copilot.md) · ★1)
- **source-spelunker**: Answers "how does this actually work" by reading real source and git history — upstream projects, nixpkgs, the kernel, vendored dependencies, store paths. Use when behaviour must be established from code rather than from documentation, and when a claim needs a file:line citation that holds up. ([ElXreno/nixos-config](https://github.com/ElXreno/nixos-config/blob/HEAD/modules/home/programs/claude-code/agents/source-spelunker.md) · ★8)
- **source-strategist**: 源材料策略专家。梳理 input 任务资料、建立事实边界、识别附件价值与缺口、形成材料地图。 ([BANG644/WebsitePresent](https://github.com/BANG644/WebsitePresent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/agents/source-strategist.md))
- **source-structure**: Run `just source-structure-check`. The required CI Source structure job runs the same checker and its tests on every PR, including guide-only edits. ([finitecomputer/finite-mono](https://github.com/finitecomputer/finite-mono/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/source-structure.md) · ★1)
- **source-structure**: Read this before you create, move or rename anything under `src/` outside `src/components/` (for components, read [component-architecture.md](component-architecture.md)). It answers three questions: **where does this file go**, **what may it import**, and **what test must it have**. ([Abbas4tech/GhostMsg](https://github.com/Abbas4tech/GhostMsg/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/source-structure.md))
- **source-summarizer**: Fetches an external source cited by a chapter (paper, blog post, news/industry article, docs/tool, dataset, talk, or book) and writes a short summary focused on WHAT IN THE SOURCE TIES BACK TO THE CHAPTER. Use during the optional Source Summaries stage of the Augmented Summary pipeline. Handles each reference type with the right strategy and never fabricates content or links. One batch of sources per call. ([Sandman-Ren/skills](https://github.com/Sandman-Ren/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/augmented-summary/agents/source-summarizer.md))
- **source-surveyor**: Survey ONE html-typed job board for openings — construct pre-filter URLs, paginate per (query × page) until empty page or max_listings, write normalized JSONL to data/listings/&lt;TS&gt;-&lt;source&gt;.jsonl. One invocation handles one source. ([samson-ailabs/Argopia](https://github.com/samson-ailabs/Argopia/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/source-surveyor.md) · ★1)

- **sources-critic**: Contradiction finder. Scans an evidence corpus for statements that cannot all be true — a rule stated two incompatible ways, disagreeing numbers, a decision that violates a stated constraint, mutually exclusive requirements, a silent reversal. Returns each conflict with its clashing statements quoted verbatim and anchored by source + loc, plus a clarifying question for the caller to put to the user. Detects and asks; never resolves, never edits the corpus. Writes the report and a machine-readable summary. Invoked with the corpus path. ([DomainDrivers/dd-ai](https://github.com/DomainDrivers/dd-ai/blob/HEAD/002/.claude/agents/sources-critic.md) · ★9)

Read-only, published documents only. Use before a parts-list milestone, before any purchase BOM goes to the owner, and when a part goes out of stock or gets an end-of-life notice. - **sous-explorer**: sous agent. - **sous-intake**: sous agent. Parses one Qonflo ticket before the planner - reads the Jira ticket (custom fields, comments), its epic, remote links and the contract search of rules/ticket-contract.md, and writes ac.txt, materials/jira.md and materials/contract.md to the ticket's blackboard. - **sous-jira**: sous agent. Creates QRD Jira tickets from drafts written on a board, and edits only tickets it created earlier. Never comments, transitions, assigns or touches other tickets. Only use when a sous runner dispatches it. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-jira.md))
- **sous-light-runner**: sous agent. Runs one small fix on a Qonflo ticket without the full pipeline - scout gate, brief, worker, verify, optional browser check, blast check, publish - by dispatching the existing sous-* agents, keeping the ledger at mode=light, and hands the ticket back to the full runner when the gate fails. Only use when the sous main session dispatches a light run. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-light-runner.md))
- **sous-planner**: sous agent. Plays S0 for one Qonflo ticket - reads the AC and materials sous-intake wrote to the ticket's blackboard and the code, maps the terrain, and writes the session prompt prompts/S&lt;n&gt;.md. Never edits code. Only use when sous-ticket-runner dispatches it. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-planner.md))
- **sous-pr-reviewer**: sous agent. Reviews a teammate's Qonflo pull request following the review-pr skill - contract first, AC to path:line, blast radius, screenshots for frontend work - and returns findings with severities and a verdict. Never edits code, never comments on the PR. Only use when sous-review-runner dispatches it. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-pr-reviewer.md))
- **sous-publisher**: sous agent. Stages a reviewed and verified Qonflo change per path, commits it, pushes its feat|fix/&lt;KEY&gt;-... branch and opens the pull request (draft when told). Only use when sous-ticket-runner dispatches a publish. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-publisher.md))
- **sous-review-runner**: sous agent. Runs one review of a teammate's Qonflo PR end to end - PR state, ledger kind=review, sous-pr-reviewer, verdict in the ledger - and returns a short verdict summary. Never comments on the PR, never approves it. Only use when the sous main session dispatches a review. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-review-runner.md))
- **sous-reviewer**: sous agent. Reviews a Qonflo ticket's change against its acceptance criteria - LOOP on one task before verification (sonnet), FINAL on the whole ticket with blast radius before publishing (runner passes model opus). Every AC line needs a path:line; FINAL findings name the task they belong to. Never edits code. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-reviewer.md))
- **sous-reviewer**: Reads `sous doctor` and `sous report` output and proposes the smallest harness change for each failure or false positive. - **sous-ticket-runner**: sous agent. Runs one Qonflo ticket end to end - intake, terrain, worktrees, tasks (worker, review loop, verify), final review, video, publish - by dispatching the other sous-* agents, keeping the ledger current with status.cjs, and returns a short summary. Only use when the sous main session dispatches a ticket. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-ticket-runner.md))
- **sous-ticket-worker**: sous agent. Implements one task of a Qonflo ticket from its blackboard session prompt inside the ticket's prepared worktree. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-ticket-worker.md))
- **sous-verifier**: sous agent. Runs the related tests, TEST-CMD lines and type checks of one task on a prepared Qonflo worktree while the runner holds `status.cjs lock verify`, then reports. Never edits code, never reads Jira, never runs git writes. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-verifier.md))

- **sous-work-runner**: sous agent. - **spec-analyst**: Analyzes specifications, PRDs, and feature descriptions to extract structured requirements, acceptance criteria, and technical constraints. Use before feature planning when requirements are vague or complex. ([rivit-studio/GIR](https://github.com/rivit-studio/GIR/blob/HEAD/plugins/gir-core/agents/spec-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **spec-analyst**: Etapa ANÁLISIS del SDD. Chequeo de consistencia read-only entre spec.md ↔ contract.md ↔ plan.md ↔ tasks.md ↔ contratos del repo, ANTES de implementar. Reporta AC sin cobertura, scope creep, asunciones de contrato no respaldadas y violaciones de CONSTITUTION/AGENTS/CLAUDE. Lo invoca el comando /analyze. No modifica nada. ([SantiDragotto/agentic-sdlc](https://github.com/SantiDragotto/agentic-sdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **spec-analyst**: Fase F1 do Workflow IA. Lê o card original (card.md) e a codebase e escreve o spec executável (spec.md com critérios CA-n em Gherkin, complexidade, risco, UI, impacto, fora de escopo, DoR) e o decisoes.md. Use quando o orquestrador delegar a extração de specs de uma task. ([4r7hur9/Simple-Click-Next.Ts](https://github.com/4r7hur9/Simple-Click-Next.Ts/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/workflow-ia/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: Answers questions about the project spec in docs/project-fa.md (Persian). Use when you need what the spec says about a concept, rule, context, requirement or scope, instead of reading the whole spec yourself. ([Amir-HShahi/petro-ops](https://github.com/Amir-HShahi/petro-ops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: ERP / supply-chain functional consultant for DiecastOS. Use BEFORE any code for a module: reads the brief and the research, reads the existing schema/code, interviews the owner, and writes the agent-layer spec docs/specs/&lt;module&gt;/spec.md (state machine, numbered business rules BR-&lt;MOD&gt;-NN with examples), the edge-case list, and last the short OWNER.md with max 7 decision cards. Trigger: write spec, functional spec, business rules, requirements, how should X work, PR/PO/GRN/approval/subcontracting/BOM/sale order design. ([ArunDoesCode/ERP-diecast](https://github.com/ArunDoesCode/ERP-diecast/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: TripPilot 요구사항 분석가 — 정본 설계 문서와 Figma 디자인을 읽고 사이클 대상 작업의 요구사항 브리프(AC 초안 포함)를 작성한다. 개발 사이클의 [인지] 단계 전담. ([ASM-TripPilot/trippilot](https://github.com/ASM-TripPilot/trippilot/blob/HEAD/frontend/.claude/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: WAP-Planer – erstellt Anforderungskatalog mit IDs, Glossar, Standardaufgaben und die Soll-Listen Anhang A–C aus dem Projektbeschrieb und den Referenzen. Nur auf Auftrag des Orchestrators. ([bubavanhalen/detachementsplaner-fdt](https://github.com/bubavanhalen/detachementsplaner-fdt/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: Validates design documents for completeness, contradiction, and ambiguity. Deploy during the DESIGN phase to review specs before user approval. ([caseyrtalbot/forge](https://github.com/caseyrtalbot/forge/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: Transforma briefs em specs Kiro (requirements EARS → design → tasks). Obrigado a perguntar antes de congelar requisitos. ([cleudice/kiro-ai-team](https://github.com/cleudice/kiro-ai-team/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: Turns a vague request, bug report or tracker ticket into a written specification with explicit scope, acceptance criteria and open questions. Use before any non-trivial implementation, and whenever a request is ambiguous enough that two engineers would build different things. Read-only - it produces a spec document, never code. ([cristiangirlea/claude-sdlc-kit](https://github.com/cristiangirlea/claude-sdlc-kit/blob/HEAD/src/agents/spec-analyst.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-analyst**: **When to use:** Turns a vague request, bug report or tracker ticket into a written specification with explicit scope, acceptance criteria and open questions. Use before any non-trivial implementation, and whenever a request is ambiguous enough that two engineers would build different things. Read-only - it produces a spec document, never code. ([cristiangirlea/claude-sdlc-kit](https://github.com/cristiangirlea/claude-sdlc-kit/blob/HEAD/adapters/codex/sdlc/references/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: Turn an approved feature request into a precise, testable, security-aware specification that another agent can implement without guessing. ([CS3227-2610-MP3-LibConnect/CS3227-2610-MP3](https://github.com/CS3227-2610-MP3-LibConnect/CS3227-2610-MP3/blob/HEAD/workflow/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: Превращает требование из docs/SPEC.md или описание от человека в файл задачи docs/tasks/T-xxx.md с проверяемыми критериями приёмки. Используй перед началом любой новой функциональности. ([DANIK20060731/TP_Project_3kurs](https://github.com/DANIK20060731/TP_Project_3kurs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: 빨래집사 작업을 시작하기 전 스펙을 분석하는 담당. docs/plan.md·docs/backlog.md·CLAUDE.md를 근거로 태스크를 잘게 분해하고, 무엇을 만들지·어디를 건드릴지·어떤 규칙을 지켜야 하는지 정리한다. 스펙에 없는 결정이 필요하면 임의로 정하지 않고 질문거리로 남긴다. 코드는 수정하지 않는 읽기 전용 분석가. ([do-ttery/AIAgentChallenge](https://github.com/do-ttery/AIAgentChallenge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-analyst.md))
- **spec-analyst**: Menganalisis implementation existing dan menyusun specification tanpa mengubah source. ([dzakysyaam/nc-mulia](https://github.com/dzakysyaam/nc-mulia/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-analyst.md))
